# Infinity Rules: 400 New Canonical Questions

This expansion contains **400 canonical rulings** and **2000 searchable question phrasings**.
All 400 answers are classified as explicit rules answers; no interpretive answers require reviewer approval. No paid model calls were used.

## 1. When can a Trooper use Alert?

**Answer — EXPLICIT RULES ANSWER:** A Trooper may use Alert if it was not activated by an Order or ARO during that Order and either it or an allied Trooper in its Zone of Control was targeted by an Attack.

**Source:** Infinity Rules N5.3, p. 77 — ALERT!

**Five searchable phrasings:**

1. When can a Trooper use Alert?
2. Rules check: When can a Trooper use Alert?
3. In Infinity N5.3, when can a Trooper use Alert?
4. At the table, how should we resolve this: when can a Trooper use Alert?
5. Quick version: When can a model use Alert?

## 2. What does Alert let an eligible Trooper do?

**Answer — EXPLICIT RULES ANSWER:** At the Conclusion of the Order, after Saving Rolls, an eligible Trooper may turn on the spot to change its LoF angle. This is automatic, requires no Roll, does not change position, and generates no ARO.

**Source:** Infinity Rules N5.3, p. 77 — ALERT!

**Five searchable phrasings:**

1. What does Alert let an eligible Trooper do?
2. Rules check: What does Alert let an eligible Trooper do?
3. In Infinity N5.3, what does Alert let an eligible Trooper do?
4. At the table, how should we resolve this: what does Alert let an eligible Trooper do?
5. Quick version: What does Alert let an eligible model do?

## 3. After failing to Discover a Marker, when may that same Trooper try again against it?

**Answer — EXPLICIT RULES ANSWER:** That Trooper cannot attempt to Discover the same Marker again until the next Player Turn. It may still Discover a different Marker, and a different friendly Trooper may attempt to Discover the original Marker.

**Source:** Infinity Rules N5.3, p. 77 — DISCOVER

**Five searchable phrasings:**

1. After failing to Discover a Marker, when may that same Trooper try again against it?

4. At the table, how should we resolve this: after failing to Discover a Marker, when may that same Trooper try again against it?
5. Quick version: After failing to Discover a Marker, when may that same model try again against it?

## 4. If a revealed Trooper enters Camouflaged or Impersonation State again, is it the same Marker for the failed-Discover restriction?

**Answer — EXPLICIT RULES ANSWER:** No. A Trooper that was revealed and later re-entered Camouflaged State or Impersonation-1 or Impersonation-2 State does not count as the same Marker for that restriction.

**Source:** Infinity Rules N5.3, p. 77 — DISCOVER

**Five searchable phrasings:**

5. Quick version: If a revealed model enters Camouflaged or Impersonation State again, is it the same Marker for the failed-Discover restriction?

## 5. Can an Active Trooper Discover one target and Attack a different target in the same Order?

**Answer — EXPLICIT RULES ANSWER:** Yes. Discover is a Basic Short Skill and may be combined with another Basic Short Skill or Short Skill; the Active Trooper may Discover one target and Attack a different target.

**Source:** Infinity Rules N5.3, p. 77 — DISCOVER

## 6. What happens if a declared Skill fails its Requirements at Resolution?

**Answer — EXPLICIT RULES ANSWER:** The Trooper performs Idle instead. If the failed Skill used a Disposable weapon or piece of Equipment, the declared use is still expended; the Trooper also loses Camouflaged or Impersonation State if the failed declaration would normally cancel that State.

**Source:** Infinity Rules N5.3, p. 80 — IDLE

5. Quick version: What happens if a declared Skill fails its Requirements at Resolution?

## 7. What does Look Out allow friendly Troopers to do?

**Answer — EXPLICIT RULES ANSWER:** Look Out allows the declaring player's other Troopers to modify their LoF without moving. Each modification counts as that Trooper's ARO and is resolved with a Normal or Face to Face Dodge (PH-3) Roll as applicable.

**Source:** Infinity Rules N5.3, p. 81 — LOOK OUT!

4. At the table, how should we resolve this: what does Look Out allow friendly Troopers to do?
5. Quick version: What does Look Out allow friendly models to do?

## 8. What must a Trooper see to declare Look Out?

**Answer — EXPLICIT RULES ANSWER:** The user must have LoF to an enemy Trooper or Marker that declares or performs an Order. The other friendly Troopers being alerted do not need LoF to that enemy to make their Look Out Dodge (PH-3) Roll.

**Source:** Infinity Rules N5.3, p. 81 — LOOK OUT!

5. Quick version: What must a model see to declare Look Out?

## 9. Where may a Trooper place a Deployable during the Active Turn?

**Answer — EXPLICIT RULES ANSWER:** The Token or Model may be placed in Silhouette contact with the user or, if the user moved during the Order, in Silhouette contact with any part of the user's route. It must be fully supported and cannot be placed on a vertical surface.

**Source:** Infinity Rules N5.3, p. 82 — PLACE DEPLOYABLE

5. Quick version: Where may a model place a Deployable during the Active Turn?

## 10. What are the Reactive Turn requirements for Place Deployable?

**Answer — EXPLICIT RULES ANSWER:** The user needs LoF to the Active Trooper and must place the Deployable in Silhouette contact with the user. The placed item appears at the Conclusion of the Order or ARO.

**Source:** Infinity Rules N5.3, p. 82 — PLACE DEPLOYABLE

## 11. When does a newly placed Deployable become active?

**Answer — EXPLICIT RULES ANSWER:** A Deployable placed with Place Deployable is put on the table at the Conclusion of the Order or ARO. Once it is on the table, it becomes active and may function in later Orders and AROs.

**Source:** Infinity Rules N5.3, p. 82 — PLACE DEPLOYABLE

## 12. What does a Trooper need in order to declare Reload?

**Answer — EXPLICIT RULES ANSWER:** The Trooper must be in Unloaded State or have spent uses of a Disposable weapon or piece of Equipment, and must be inside the Zone of Control of a non-Null allied Trooper or game element with Baggage.

**Source:** Infinity Rules N5.3, p. 83 — RELOAD

5. Quick version: What does a model need in order to declare Reload?

## 13. What does Reload restore?

**Answer — EXPLICIT RULES ANSWER:** Without a Roll, Reload cancels the user's Unloaded State or restores all expended uses of its Disposable weapons and Equipment. Only one allied Trooper may receive this effect per Order.

**Source:** Infinity Rules N5.3, p. 83 — RELOAD

5. Quick version: What does Reload restore?

## 14. Does Reload recover Deployable weapons or Equipment already placed on the table?

**Answer — EXPLICIT RULES ANSWER:** No. Restoring Disposable uses does not affect weapons or Equipment already deployed on the table.

**Source:** Infinity Rules N5.3, p. 83 — RELOAD

## 15. When does a placed FastPanda's Repeater become usable?

**Answer — EXPLICIT RULES ANSWER:** A FastPanda placed during an Order becomes active when that Order concludes. From that point, its Repeater may be used to make Attacks through the Hacking Area.

**Source:** Infinity Rules N5.3, p. 83 — FASTPANDA

## 16. Does picking up a Speedball item cancel a Marker State?

**Answer — EXPLICIT RULES ANSWER:** Yes. When an allied non-Null Trooper enters Silhouette contact and picks up a Speedball item, that action automatically cancels any Marker State the Trooper is in.

**Source:** Infinity Rules N5.3, p. 84 — REQUEST SPEEDBALL

## 17. How many Speedball Item Tokens may one Trooper carry?

**Answer — EXPLICIT RULES ANSWER:** A Trooper cannot have more than one Speedball Item Token at a time. Speedball items are single-use and non-transferable.

**Source:** Infinity Rules N5.3, p. 84 — REQUEST SPEEDBALL

## 18. Can Speedball items be used during the Reactive Turn?

**Answer — EXPLICIT RULES ANSWER:** Yes, unless an item's own rules say otherwise. Speedball items may be used in either the Active or Reactive Turn and are removed after use.

**Source:** Infinity Rules N5.3, p. 84 — REQUEST SPEEDBALL

## 19. What modifiers apply to a Speculative Attack?

**Answer — EXPLICIT RULES ANSWER:** Apply the Speculative Attack MOD of -6 and the weapon's Range MOD. Do not apply other negative MODs such as Mimetism, Partial Cover, or Visibility Zones.

**Source:** Infinity Rules N5.3, p. 48 — SPECULATIVE ATTACK

5. Quick version: What modifiers apply to a Speculative Attack?

## 20. What are the Burst and LoF rules for Speculative Attack?

**Answer — EXPLICIT RULES ANSWER:** Speculative Attack is a Long Skill using a weapon with the Speculative Attack Trait. It may attack a target outside LoF, its Burst is always 1 regardless of the weapon or Burst MODs, and a valid trajectory to the impact point must exist.

**Source:** Infinity Rules N5.3, p. 48 — SPECULATIVE ATTACK

## 41. Under CUBE 2.0, what happens when using the Doctor Special Skill (or other Special Skills or pieces of Equipment that explicitly say so) to heal a Trooper with a Cube 2.0?

**Answer — EXPLICIT RULES ANSWER:** When using the Doctor Special Skill (or other Special Skills or pieces of Equipment that explicitly say so) to heal a Trooper with a Cube 2.0, you can expend Command Tokens to re-roll a failed WIP Roll.

## 133. What is ZONE OF CONTROL?

**Answer — EXPLICIT RULES ANSWER:** Maybe you’re wondering when, or how, exactly you can check the Zone of Control. It’s easy: once the Active Model has been placed at the end point of its movement, the two players can measure the Zone of Control along the entire route that was traced on the game board, from its starting point to its final position, these both included. This is the most natural and convenient way to check the Control Zone.

## 182. What does SIXTH SENSE allow?

**Answer — EXPLICIT RULES ANSWER:** Allows the user to respond to Attacks (and only Attacks) directed at them by enemies outside their LoF. For the purposes of drawing Line of Fire to the attackers, the user has a 360° LoF arc, and if they are in Engaged State, they can draw LoF to attackers outside their Close Combat.

**Answer — EXPLICIT RULES ANSWER:** When Frenzy Troopers gain the Impetuous Special Skill, they cancel any Marker State they are in (Camouflaged, Impersonation...), and any other States that say so. Sibylla explains The Requirements for this Special Skill demand that the Frenzy Trooper must inflict the Wound directly on the target, or cause them to enter Dead State.
